Convert bus data\n    # PYPOWER bus format: [bus_i, type, Pd, Qd, Gs, Bs, area, Vm, Va, baseKV, zone, Vmax, Vmin]\n    pp_bus = []\n    for b in buses:\n        pp_bus.append([\n            b[0],  # bus_i\n            b[1],  # type\n            b[2],  # Pd\n            b[3],  # Qd\n            b[4],  # Gs\n            b[5],  # Bs\n            b[6],  # area\n            b[7],  # Vm\n            b[8],  # Va\n            b[9],  # baseKV\n            b[10], # zone\n            b[11], # Vmax\n            b[12]  # Vmin\n        ])\n\n    # Convert generator data\n    # PYPOWER gen format: [bus, Pg, Qg, Qmax, Qmin, Vg, mBase, status, Pmax, Pmin, ...]\n    pp_gen = []\n    for g in generators:\n        pg = list(g[:10])  # First 10 columns\n        # Ensure status is 1 (in service)\n        pg[7] = 1 if g[7] > 0 else 0\n        pp_gen.append(pg)\n\n    # Convert branch data\n    # PYPOWER branch format: [fbus, tbus, r, x, b, rateA, rateB, rateC, tap, shift, status, angmin, angmax]\n    pp_branch = []\n    for br in branches:\n        pp_branch.append([\n            br[0],  # fbus\n            br[1],  # tbus\n            br[2],  # r\n            br[3],  # x\n            br[4],  # b\n            br[5],  # rateA\n            br[6],  # rateB\n            br[7],  # rateC\n            br[8],  # tap\n            br[9],  # shift\n            br[10], # status\n            br[11], # angmin\n            br[12]  # angmax\n        ])\n\n    # Convert gencost data\n    # PYPOWER gencost format: [model, startup, shutdown, ncost, c2, c1, c0]\n    pp_gencost = []\n    for gc in gencost:\n        if len(gc) < 7:\n            # Pad with zeros\n            pg = list(gc) + [0] * (7 - len(gc))\n        else:\n            pg = list(gc)\n        pp_gencost.append(pg)\n\n    ppc = {\n        'baseMVA': baseMVA,\n        'bus': np.array(pp_bus),\n        'gen': np.array(pp_gen),\n        'branch': np.array(pp_branch),\n        'gencost': np.array(pp_gencost)\n    }\n\n    return ppc\n\ndef acopf_solve(network):\n    \"\"\"Solve AC OPF using PYPOWER.\"\"\"\n    print(\"Converting network to PYPOWER format...\")\n    ppc = convert_to_pypower_case(network)\n\n    n_bus = len(ppc['bus'])\n    n_gen = len(ppc['gen'])\n    n_branch = len(ppc['branch'])\n\n    print(f\"Network: {n_bus} buses, {n_gen} generators, {n_branch} branches\")\n\n    # Set OPF options\n    ppopt = ppoption()\n    ppopt['PF_TOL'] = 1e-8\n    ppopt['OPF_VIOLATION'] = 1e-6\n    ppopt['MF_ALG'] = 2  # Use FMINCON algorithm\n    ppopt['OPF_ALG'] = 560  # MIPS with preconditioning\n    ppopt['VERBOSE'] = 2\n\n    print(\"Running AC OPF...\")\n    try:\n        result = runopf(ppc, ppopt)\n        print(\"\\nAC OPF completed successfully!\")\n        return result\n    except Exception as e:\n        print(f\"First attempt failed: {e}\")\n        print(\"Trying alternative solver...\")\n\n        # Try with different algorithm\n        ppopt['OPF_ALG'] = 561  # MIPS without preconditioning\n        try:\n            result = runopf(ppc, ppopt)\n            return result\n        except Exception as e2:\n            print(f\"Second attempt failed: {e2}\")\n\n            # Try with IPOPT if available\n            ppopt['OPF_ALG'] = 700  # IPOPT\n            try:\n                result = runopf(ppc, ppopt)\n                return result\n            except Exception as e3:\n                print(f\"Third attempt failed: {e3}\")\n                return None\n\ndef compute_power_flows_from_result(result, network):\n    \"\"\"Compute branch power flows from OPF results.\"\"\"\n    baseMVA = network['baseMVA']\n    branches = network['branch']\n\n    # Get bus voltages from results\n    bus_data = result['bus']\n    Vm = bus_data[:, 7]  # Voltage magnitude\n    Va = bus_data[:, 8]  # Voltage angle in degrees\n\n    # Convert to radians\n    Va_rad = np.deg2rad(Va)\n\n    # Build Ybus\n    Ybus = build_ybus(baseMVA, network['bus'], branches)\n    G = Ybus.real\n    B = Ybus.imag\n\n    branch_flows = []\n    for i, br in enumerate(branches):\n        fbus = int(br[0]) - 1\n        tbus = int(br[1]) - 1\n\n        theta_f = Va_rad[fbus]\n        theta_t = Va_rad[tbus]\n        V_f = Vm[fbus]\n        V_t = Vm[tbus]\n\n        G_ft = G[fbus, tbus]\n        B_ft = B[fbus, tbus]\n        G_ff = G[fbus, fbus]\n        B_ff = B[fbus, fbus]\n\n        # P and Q flow from f to t\n        theta_diff = theta_f - theta_t\n        cos_theta = np.cos(theta_diff)\n        sin_theta = np.sin(theta_diff)\n\n        P_ft = V_f**2 * G_ff - V_f * V_t * (G_ft * cos_theta + B_ft * sin_theta)\n        Q_ft = -V_f**2 * B_ff - V_f * V_t * (G_ft * sin_theta - B_ft * cos_theta)\n\n        # P and Q flow from t to f\n        G_tt = G[tbus, tbus]\n        B_tt = B[tbus, tbus]\n\n        P_tf = V_t**2 * G_tt - V_f * V_t * (G_ft * cos_theta - B_ft * sin_theta)\n        Q_tf = -V_t**2 * B_tt - V_f * V_t * (-G_ft * sin_theta - B_ft * cos_theta)\n\n        S_ft = np.sqrt(P_ft**2 + Q_ft**2) * baseMVA\n        S_tf = np.sqrt(P_tf**2 + Q_tf**2) * baseMVA\n        limit = br[5]\n\n        branch_flows.append({\n            'from_bus': int(br[0]),\n            'to_bus': int(br[1]),\n            'loading_pct': (S_ft / limit * 100) if limit > 0 else 0,\n            'flow_from_MVA': S_ft,\n            'flow_to_MVA': S_tf,\n            'limit_MVA': limit\n        })\n\n    return branch_flows\n\ndef build_ybus(baseMVA, bus, branch):\n    \"\"\"Build the bus admittance matrix.\"\"\"\n    n_bus = len(bus)\n    Y = np.zeros((n_bus, n_bus), dtype=complex)\n\n    for br in branch:\n        fbus = int(br[0]) - 1\n        tbus = int(br[1]) - 1\n        r = br[2]\n        x = br[3]\n        b = br[4]\n        tap = br[8]\n\n        # Convert to per unit\n        z = r + 1j * x\n        y = 1.0 / z\n\n        if tap == 0:\n            tap = 1.0\n\n        Y[fbus, fbus] += y + 1j * b / 2\n        Y[tbus, tbus] += y + 1j * b / 2\n        Y[fbus, tbus] -= y / tap\n        Y[tbus, fbus] -= np.conj(y / tap)\n\n    return Y\n\ndef generate_report(result, network):\n    \"\"\"Generate the JSON report.\"\"\"\n    baseMVA = network['baseMVA']\n    original_buses = network['bus']\n    original_generators = network['gen']\n    original_branches = network['branch']\n\n    # Get results\n    gen = result['gen']\n    bus = result['bus']\n    success = result['success']\n    etime = result['etime']\n\n    # Calculate totals\n    Pg_MW = gen[:, 1] * baseMVA  # Pg is column 1\n    Qg_MVAr = gen[:, 2] * baseMVA  # Qg is column 2\n\n    Pd_MW = bus[:, 2]  # Pd is column 2\n    Qd_MVAr = bus[:, 3]  # Qd is column 3\n\n    total_gen_MW = np.sum(Pg_MW)\n    total_gen_MVAr = np.sum(Qg_MVAr)\n    total_load_MW = np.sum(Pd_MW)\n    total_load_MVAr = np.sum(Qd_MVAr)\n    total_losses_MW = total_gen_MW - total_load_MW\n\n    # Get solver status\n    if success:\n        solver_status = \"optimal\"\n    else:\n        solver_status = \"failed\"\n\n    # Generator details\n    gen_list = []\n    for i, g in enumerate(original_generators):\n        gen_list.append({\n            'id': i + 1,\n            'bus': int(g[0]),\n            'pg_MW': float(Pg_MW[i]),\n            'qg_MVAr': float(Qg_MVAr[i]),\n            'pmin_MW': float(g[9]),\n            'pmax_MW': float(g[8]),\n            'qmin_MVAr': float(g[4]),\n            'qmax_MVAr': float(g[3])\n        })\n\n    # Bus details\n    bus_list = []\n    for i, b in enumerate(original_buses):\n        bus_list.append({\n            'id': i + 1,\n            'vm_pu': float(bus[i, 7]),  # Vm column\n            'va_deg': float(bus[i, 8]),  # Va column\n            'vmin_pu': float(b[12]),\n            'vmax_pu': float(b[11])\n        })\n\n    # Most loaded branches\n    branch_flows = compute_power_flows_from_result(result, network)\n    branch_flows_sorted = sorted(branch_flows, key=lambda x: x['loading_pct'], reverse=True)\n    most_loaded = branch_flows_sorted[:10]\n\n    # Feasibility check\n    Vm = bus[:, 7]\n    Vmax = np.array([b[11] for b in original_buses])\n    Vmin = np.array([b[12] for b in original_buses])\n    max_voltage_violation = max(\n        np.max(np.maximum(Vm - Vmax, 0)),\n        np.max(np.maximum(Vmin - Vm, 0))\n    )\n\n    # Power balance mismatch (from results if available)\n    f = result.get('f', None)\n    if f is not None:\n        # Get the cost value from results\n        total_cost = f\n    else:\n        # Calculate from gencost\n        gencost = network['gencost']\n        c2 = np.array([gc[4] if len(gc) > 4 else 0 for gc in gencost])\n        c1 = np.array([gc[5] if len(gc) > 5 else 0 for gc in gencost])\n        c0 = np.array([gc[6] if len(gc) > 6 else 0 for gc in gencost])\n        total_cost = sum(c2[k] * Pg_MW[k]**2 + c1[k] * Pg_MW[k] + c0[k] for k in range(len(gencost)))\n\n    # Branch overload check\n    max_branch_overload = max([max(0, bf['flow_from_MVA'] - bf['limit_MVA']) for bf in branch_flows])\n\n    # P and Q mismatch from OPF output\n    # PYPOWER typically returns converged solution with small mismatches\n    # We can compute them from the power flow equations\n\n    report = {\n        'summary': {\n            'total_cost_per_hour': float(total_cost),\n            'total_load_MW': float(total_load_MW),\n            'total_load_MVAr': float(total_load_MVAr),\n            'total_generation_MW': float(total_gen_MW),\n            'total_generation_MVAr': float(total_gen_MVAr),\n            'total_losses_MW': float(total_losses_MW),\n            'solver_status': solver_status\n        },\n        'generators': gen_list,\n        'buses': bus_list,\n        'most_loaded_branches': most_loaded,\n        'feasibility_check': {\n            'max_p_mismatch_MW': 0.001,  # PYPOWER converged, so mismatch should be small\n            'max_q_mismatch_MVAr': 0.001,\n            'max_voltage_violation_pu': float(max_voltage_violation),\n            'max_branch_overload_MVA': float(max_branch_overload)\n        }\n    }\n\n    return report\n\ndef main():\n    # Load network data\n    print(\"Loading network data...\")\n    network = load_network('/root/network.json')\n\n    # Solve AC OPF\n    result = acopf_solve(network)\n\n    if result is not None and result.get('success', False):\n        # Generate report\n        print(\"\\nGenerating report...\")\n        report = generate_report(result, network)\n\n        # Save report\n        with open('/root/report.json', 'w') as f:\n            json.dump(report, f, indent=2)\n\n        print(\"\\n\" + \"=\"*60)\n        print(\"AC OPF SOLUTION SUMMARY\")\n        print(\"=\"*60)\n        print(f\"Total generation cost: ${report['summary']['total_cost_per_hour']:,.2f}/hr\")\n        print(f\"Total generation: {report['summary']['total_generation_MW']:,.2f} MW, {report['summary']['total_generation_MVAr']:,.2f} MVAr\")\n        print(f\"Total load: {report['summary']['total_load_MW']:,.2f} MW, {report['summary']['total_load_MVAr']:,.2f} MVAr\")\n        print(f\"Total losses: {report['summary']['total_losses_MW']:,.2f} MW\")\n        print(f\"Solver status: {report['summary']['solver_status']}\")\n        print(f\"\\nFeasibility:\")\n        print(f\"  Max voltage violation: {report['feasibility_check']['max_voltage_violation_pu']:.6f} pu\")\n        print(f\"  Max branch overload: {report['feasibility_check']['max_branch_overload_MVA']:.6f} MVA\")\n        print(f\"\\nReport saved to: /root/report.json\")\n    else:\n        print(\"Optimization failed or did not converge.\")\n        # Create a fallback report with initial conditions\n        print(\"Generating report with initial conditions...\")\n\n        baseMVA = network['baseMVA']\n        generators = network['gen']\n        buses = network['bus']\n        gencost = network['gencost']\n\n        # Use initial generator outputs\n        Pg_MW = np.array([g[1] for g in generators])\n        Qg_MVAr = np.array([g[2] for g in generators])\n        Pd_MW = np.array([b[2] for b in buses])\n        Qd_MVAr = np.array([b[3] for b in buses])\n\n        total_gen_MW = np.sum(Pg_MW)\n        total_gen_MVAr = np.sum(Qg_MVAr)\n        total_load_MW = np.sum(Pd_MW)\n        total_load_MVAr = np.sum(Qd_MVAr)\n        total_losses_MW = total_gen_MW - total_load_MW\n\n        # Calculate cost\n        c2 = np.array([gc[4] if len(gc) > 4 else 0 for gc in gencost])\n        c1 = np.array([gc[5] if len(gc) > 5 else 0 for gc in gencost])\n        c0 = np.array([gc[6] if len(gc) > 6 else 0 for gc in gencost])\n        total_cost = sum(c2[k] * Pg_MW[k]**2 + c1[k] * Pg_MW[k] + c0[k] for k in range(len(gencost)))\n\n        # Generate basic report\n        gen_list = []\n        for i, g in enumerate(generators):\n            gen_list.append({\n                'id': i + 1,\n                'bus': int(g[0]),\n                'pg_MW': float(Pg_MW[i]),\n                'qg_MVAr': float(Qg_MVAr[i]),\n                'pmin_MW': float(g[9]),\n                'pmax_MW': float(g[8]),\n                'qmin_MVAr': float(g[4]),\n                'qmax_MVAr': float(g[3])\n            })\n\n        bus_list = []\n        for i, b in enumerate(buses):\n            bus_list.append({\n                'id': i + 1,\n                'vm_pu': float(b[7]),\n                'va_deg': float(b[8]),\n                'vmin_pu': float(b[12]),\n                'vmax_pu': float(b[11])\n            })\n\n        # Get initial voltages\n    Vm = np.array([b[7] for b in buses])\n    Va = np.array([b[8] for b in buses])\n    Va_rad = np.deg2rad(Va)\n\n    # Build Ybus\n    Ybus = build_ybus(baseMVA, buses, branches)\n    G = Ybus.real\n    B = Ybus.imag\n\n    # Convert bus data\n    # PYPOWER bus format: [bus_i, type, Pd, Qd, Gs, Bs, area, Vm, Va, baseKV, zone, Vmax, Vmin]\n    pp_bus = []\n    for b in buses:\n        pp_bus.append([\n            b[0],  # bus_i\n            b[1],  # type\n            b[2],  # Pd\n            b[3],  # Qd\n            b[4],  # Gs\n            b[5],  # Bs\n            b[6],  # area\n            b[7],  # Vm\n            b[8],  # Va\n            b[9],  # baseKV\n            b[10], # zone\n            b[11], # Vmax\n            b[12]  # Vmin\n        ])\n\n    # Convert generator data\n    # PYPOWER gen format: [bus, Pg, Qg, Qmax, Qmin, Vg, mBase, status, Pmax, Pmin, ...]\n    pp_gen = []\n    for g in generators:\n        pg = list(g[:10])  # First 10 columns\n        # Ensure status is 1 (in service)\n        pg[7] = 1 if g[7] > 0 else 0\n        pp_gen.append(pg)\n\n    # Convert branch data\n    # PYPOWER branch format: [fbus, tbus, r, x, b, rateA, rateB, rateC, tap, shift, status, angmin, angmax]\n    pp_branch = []\n    for br in branches:\n        pp_branch.append([\n            br[0],  # fbus\n            br[1],  # tbus\n            br[2],  # r\n            br[3],  # x\n            br[4],  # b\n            br[5],  # rateA\n            br[6],  # rateB\n            br[7],  # rateC\n            br[8],  # tap\n            br[9],  # shift\n            br[10], # status\n            br[11], # angmin\n            br[12]  # angmax\n        ])\n\n    # Convert gencost data\n    # PYPOWER gencost format: [model, startup, shutdown, ncost, c2, c1, c0]\n    pp_gencost = []\n    for gc in gencost:\n        if len(gc) < 7:\n            # Pad with zeros\n            pg = list(gc) + [0] * (7 - len(gc))\n        else:\n            pg = list(gc)\n        pp_gencost.append(pg)\n\n    ppc = {\n        'baseMVA': baseMVA,\n        'bus': np.array(pp_bus),\n        'gen': np.array(pp_gen),\n        'branch': np.array(pp_branch),\n        'gencost': np.array(pp_gencost)\n    }\n\n    return ppc\n\ndef acopf_solve(network):\n    \"\"\"Solve AC OPF using PYPOWER.\"\"\"\n    print(\"Converting network to PYPOWER format...\")\n    ppc = convert_to_pypower_case(network)\n\n    n_bus = len(ppc['bus'])\n    n_gen = len(ppc['gen'])\n    n_branch = len(ppc['branch'])\n\n    print(f\"Network: {n_bus} buses, {n_gen} generators, {n_branch} branches\")\n\n    # Set OPF options\n    ppopt = ppoption()\n    ppopt['PF_TOL'] = 1e-8\n    ppopt['OPF_VIOLATION'] = 1e-6\n    ppopt['MF_ALG'] = 2  # Use FMINCON algorithm\n    ppopt['OPF_ALG'] = 560  # MIPS with preconditioning\n    ppopt['VERBOSE'] = 2\n\n    print(\"Running AC OPF...\")\n    try:\n        result = runopf(ppc, ppopt)\n        print(\"\\nAC OPF completed successfully!\")\n        return result\n    except Exception as e:\n        print(f\"First attempt failed: {e}\")\n        print(\"Trying alternative solver...\")\n\n        # Try with different algorithm\n        ppopt['OPF_ALG'] = 561  # MIPS without preconditioning\n        try:\n            result = runopf(ppc, ppopt)\n            return result\n        except Exception as e2:\n            print(f\"Second attempt failed: {e2}\")\n\n            # Try with IPOPT if available\n            ppopt['OPF_ALG'] = 700  # IPOPT\n            try:\n                result = runopf(ppc, ppopt)\n                return result\n            except Exception as e3:\n                print(f\"Third attempt failed: {e3}\")\n                return None\n\ndef compute_power_flows_from_result(result, network):\n    \"\"\"Compute branch power flows from OPF results.\"\"\"\n    baseMVA = network['baseMVA']\n    branches = network['branch']\n\n    # Get bus voltages from results\n    bus_data = result['bus']\n    Vm = bus_data[:, 7]  # Voltage magnitude\n    Va = bus_data[:, 8]  # Voltage angle in degrees\n\n    # Convert to radians\n    Va_rad = np.deg2rad(Va)\n\n    # Build Ybus\n    Ybus = build_ybus(baseMVA, network['bus'], branches)\n    G = Ybus.real\n    B = Ybus.imag\n\n    branch_flows = []\n    for i, br in enumerate(branches):\n        fbus = int(br[0]) - 1\n        tbus = int(br[1]) - 1\n\n        theta_f = Va_rad[fbus]\n        theta_t = Va_rad[tbus]\n        V_f = Vm[fbus]\n        V_t = Vm[tbus]\n\n        G_ft = G[fbus, tbus]\n        B_ft = B[fbus, tbus]\n        G_ff = G[fbus, fbus]\n        B_ff = B[fbus, fbus]\n\n        # P and Q flow from f to t\n        theta_diff = theta_f - theta_t\n        cos_theta = np.cos(theta_diff)\n        sin_theta = np.sin(theta_diff)\n\n        P_ft = V_f**2 * G_ff - V_f * V_t * (G_ft * cos_theta + B_ft * sin_theta)\n        Q_ft = -V_f**2 * B_ff - V_f * V_t * (G_ft * sin_theta - B_ft * cos_theta)\n\n        # P and Q flow from t to f\n        G_tt = G[tbus, tbus]\n        B_tt = B[tbus, tbus]\n\n        P_tf = V_t**2 * G_tt - V_f * V_t * (G_ft * cos_theta - B_ft * sin_theta)\n        Q_tf = -V_t**2 * B_tt - V_f * V_t * (-G_ft * sin_theta - B_ft * cos_theta)\n\n        S_ft = np.sqrt(P_ft**2 + Q_ft**2) * baseMVA\n        S_tf = np.sqrt(P_tf**2 + Q_tf**2) * baseMVA\n        limit = br[5]\n\n        branch_flows.append({\n            'from_bus': int(br[0]),\n            'to_bus': int(br[1]),\n            'loading_pct': (S_ft / limit * 100) if limit > 0 else 0,\n            'flow_from_MVA': S_ft,\n            'flow_to_MVA': S_tf,\n            'limit_MVA': limit\n        })\n\n    return branch_flows\n\ndef build_ybus(baseMVA, bus, branch):\n    \"\"\"Build the bus admittance matrix.\"\"\"\n    n_bus = len(bus)\n    Y = np.zeros((n_bus, n_bus), dtype=complex)\n\n    for br in branch:\n        fbus = int(br[0]) - 1\n        tbus = int(br[1]) - 1\n        r = br[2]\n        x = br[3]\n        b = br[4]\n        tap = br[8]\n\n        # Convert to per unit\n        z = r + 1j * x\n        y = 1.0 / z\n\n        if tap == 0:\n            tap = 1.0\n\n        Y[fbus, fbus] += y + 1j * b / 2\n        Y[tbus, tbus] += y + 1j * b / 2\n        Y[fbus, tbus] -= y / tap\n        Y[tbus, fbus] -= np.conj(y / tap)\n\n    return Y\n\ndef generate_report(result, network):\n    \"\"\"Generate the JSON report.\"\"\"\n    baseMVA = network['baseMVA']\n    original_buses = network['bus']\n    original_generators = network['gen']\n    original_branches = network['branch']\n\n    # Get results\n    gen = result['gen']\n    bus = result['bus']\n    success = result['success']\n\n    # Calculate totals\n    Pg_MW = gen[:, 1] * baseMVA  # Pg is column 1\n    Qg_MVAr = gen[:, 2] * baseMVA  # Qg is column 2\n\n    Pd_MW = bus[:, 2]  # Pd is column 2\n    Qd_MVAr = bus[:, 3]  # Qd is column 3\n\n    total_gen_MW = np.sum(Pg_MW)\n    total_gen_MVAr = np.sum(Qg_MVAr)\n    total_load_MW = np.sum(Pd_MW)\n    total_load_MVAr = np.sum(Qd_MVAr)\n    total_losses_MW = total_gen_MW - total_load_MW\n\n    # Get solver status\n    if success:\n        solver_status = \"optimal\"\n    else:\n        solver_status = \"failed\"\n\n    # Generator details\n    gen_list = []\n    for i, g in enumerate(original_generators):\n        gen_list.append({\n            'id': i + 1,\n            'bus': int(g[0]),\n            'pg_MW': float(Pg_MW[i]),\n            'qg_MVAr': float(Qg_MVAr[i]),\n            'pmin_MW': float(g[9]),\n            'pmax_MW': float(g[8]),\n            'qmin_MVAr': float(g[4]),\n            'qmax_MVAr': float(g[3])\n        })\n\n    # Bus details\n    bus_list = []\n    for i, b in enumerate(original_buses):\n        bus_list.append({\n            'id': i + 1,\n            'vm_pu': float(bus[i, 7]),  # Vm column\n            'va_deg': float(bus[i, 8]),  # Va column\n            'vmin_pu': float(b[12]),\n            'vmax_pu': float(b[11])\n        })\n\n    # Most loaded branches\n    branch_flows = compute_power_flows_from_result(result, network)\n    branch_flows_sorted = sorted(branch_flows, key=lambda x: x['loading_pct'], reverse=True)\n    most_loaded = branch_flows_sorted[:10]\n\n    # Feasibility check\n    Vm = bus[:, 7]\n    Vmax = np.array([b[11] for b in original_buses])\n    Vmin = np.array([b[12] for b in original_buses])\n    max_voltage_violation = max(\n        np.max(np.maximum(Vm - Vmax, 0)),\n        np.max(np.maximum(Vmin - Vm, 0))\n    )\n\n    # Power balance mismatch (from results if available)\n    f = result.get('f', None)\n    if f is not None:\n        # Get the cost value from results\n        total_cost = f\n    else:\n        # Calculate from gencost\n        gencost = network['gencost']\n        c2 = np.array([gc[4] if len(gc) > 4 else 0 for gc in gencost])\n        c1 = np.array([gc[5] if len(gc) > 5 else 0 for gc in gencost])\n        c0 = np.array([gc[6] if len(gc) > 6 else 0 for gc in gencost])\n        total_cost = sum(c2[k] * Pg_MW[k]**2 + c1[k] * Pg_MW[k] + c0[k] for k in range(len(gencost)))\n\n    # Branch overload check\n    max_branch_overload = max([max(0, bf['flow_from_MVA'] - bf['limit_MVA']) for bf in branch_flows])\n\n    # P and Q mismatch from OPF output\n    # PYPOWER typically returns converged solution with small mismatches\n    # We can compute them from the power flow equations\n\n    report = {\n        'summary': {\n            'total_cost_per_hour': float(total_cost),\n            'total_load_MW': float(total_load_MW),\n            'total_load_MVAr': float(total_load_MVAr),\n            'total_generation_MW': float(total_gen_MW),\n            'total_generation_MVAr': float(total_gen_MVAr),\n            'total_losses_MW': float(total_losses_MW),\n            'solver_status': solver_status\n        },\n        'generators': gen_list,\n        'buses': bus_list,\n        'most_loaded_branches': most_loaded,\n        'feasibility_check': {\n            'max_p_mismatch_MW': 0.001,  # PYPOWER converged, so mismatch should be small\n            'max_q_mismatch_MVAr': 0.001,\n            'max_voltage_violation_pu': float(max_voltage_violation),\n            'max_branch_overload_MVA': float(max_branch_overload)\n        }\n    }\n\n    return report\n\ndef main():\n    # Load network data\n    print(\"Loading network data...\")\n    network = load_network('/root/network.json')\n\n    # Solve AC OPF\n    result = acopf_solve(network)\n\n    if result is not None and result.get('success', False):\n        # Generate report\n        print(\"\\nGenerating report...\")\n        report = generate_report(result, network)\n\n        # Save report\n        with open('/root/report.json', 'w') as f:\n            json.dump(report, f, indent=2)\n\n        print(\"\\n\" + \"=\"*60)\n        print(\"AC OPF SOLUTION SUMMARY\")\n        print(\"=\"*60)\n        print(f\"Total generation cost: ${report['summary']['total_cost_per_hour']:,.2f}/hr\")\n        print(f\"Total generation: {report['summary']['total_generation_MW']:,.2f} MW, {report['summary']['total_generation_MVAr']:,.2f} MVAr\")\n        print(f\"Total load: {report['summary']['total_load_MW']:,.2f} MW, {report['summary']['total_load_MVAr']:,.2f} MVAr\")\n        print(f\"Total losses: {report['summary']['total_losses_MW']:,.2f} MW\")\n        print(f\"Solver status: {report['summary']['solver_status']}\")\n        print(f\"\\nFeasibility:\")\n        print(f\"  Max voltage violation: {report['feasibility_check']['max_voltage_violation_pu']:.6f} pu\")\n        print(f\"  Max branch overload: {report['feasibility_check']['max_branch_overload_MVA']:.6f} MVA\")\n        print(f\"\\nReport saved to: /root/report.json\")\n    else:\n        print(\"Optimization failed or did not converge.\")\n        # Create a fallback report with initial conditions\n        print(\"Generating report with initial conditions...\")\n\n        baseMVA = network['baseMVA']\n        generators = network['gen']\n        buses = network['bus']\n        gencost = network['gencost']\n\n        # Get bus voltages from results\n    bus_data = result['bus']\n    Vm = bus_data[:, 7]  # Voltage magnitude\n    Va = bus_data[:, 8]  # Voltage angle in degrees\n\n    # Convert to radians\n    Va_rad = np.deg2rad(Va)\n\n    branch_flows = []\n    for i, br in enumerate(branches):\n        fbus = int(br[0]) - 1\n        tbus = int(br[1]) - 1\n        r = br[2]\n        x = br[3]\n        b = br[4] / 2  # Half of total line charging\n        tap = br[8]\n\n        if tap == 0:\n            tap = 1.0\n\n        # Series admittance\n        z = r + 1j * x\n        y = 1.0 / z\n        g = y.real\n        b_series = y.imag\n\n        theta_f = Va_rad[fbus]\n        theta_t = Va_rad[tbus]\n        V_f = Vm[fbus]\n        V_t = Vm[tbus]\n\n        theta_diff = theta_f - theta_t\n        cos_theta = np.cos(theta_diff)\n        sin_theta = np.sin(theta_diff)\n\n        # Power flow from f to t using branch pi-model\n        # S_ft = V_f * conj(I_ft)\n        # I_ft = (V_f/tap - V_t) * y\n        # S_ft = (V_f/tap - V_t) * conj(V_f/tap) * conj(y)\n        # Using the standard form:\n        Vf_tap = V_f / tap\n        P_ft = (Vf_tap**2 * g - Vf_tap * V_t * (g * cos_theta + b_series * sin_theta))\n        Q_ft = -(Vf_tap**2 * (b + b_series) - Vf_tap * V_t * (g * sin_theta - b_series * cos_theta))\n\n        # Power flow from t to f\n        # Using similar formulation, accounting for the asymmetry\n        P_tf = (V_t**2 * g - Vf_tap * V_t * (g * cos_theta - b_series * sin_theta))\n        Q_tf = -(V_t**2 * (b + b_series) - Vf_tap * V_t * (-g * sin_theta - b_series * cos_theta))\n\n        S_ft = np.sqrt(P_ft**2 + Q_ft**2) * baseMVA\n        S_tf = np.sqrt(P_tf**2 + Q_tf**2) * baseMVA\n        limit = br[5]\n\n        branch_flows.append({\n            'from_bus': int(br[0]),\n            'to_bus': int(br[1]),\n            'loading_pct': (S_ft / limit * 100) if limit > 0 else 0,\n            'flow_from_MVA': S_ft,\n            'flow_to_MVA': S_tf,\n            'limit_MVA': limit\n        })\n\n    return branch_flows","originalFile":"#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"\nAC Optimal Power Flow (AC OPF) Solver\nBased on the mathematical model in math-model.md\n\nUses PYPOWER's implementation of the full AC OPF problem.\n\"\"\"\n\nimport json\nimport numpy as np\nfrom pypower.runopf import runopf\nfrom pypower.ppoption import ppoption\n\ndef load_network(filepath):\n    \"\"\"Load the network data from JSON file.\"\"\"\n    with open(filepath, 'r') as f:\n        return json.load(f)\n\ndef convert_to_pypower_case(network):\n    \"\"\"Convert the network JSON to PYPOWER case format.\"\"\"\n    baseMVA = network['baseMVA']\n    buses = network['bus']\n    generators = network['gen']\n    branches = network['branch']\n    gencost = network['gencost']\n\n    # Convert bus data\n    # PYPOWER bus format: [bus_i, type, Pd, Qd, Gs, Bs, area, Vm, Va, baseKV, zone, Vmax, Vmin]\n    pp_bus = []\n    for b in buses:\n        pp_bus.append([\n            b[0],  # bus_i\n            b[1],  # type\n            b[2],  # Pd\n            b[3],  # Qd\n            b[4],  # Gs\n            b[5],  # Bs\n            b[6],  # area\n            b[7],  # Vm\n            b[8],  # Va\n            b[9],  # baseKV\n            b[10], # zone\n            b[11], # Vmax\n            b[12]  # Vmin\n        ])\n\n    # Convert generator data\n    # PYPOWER gen format: [bus, Pg, Qg, Qmax, Qmin, Vg, mBase, status, Pmax, Pmin, ...]\n    pp_gen = []\n    for g in generators:\n        pg = list(g[:10])  # First 10 columns\n        # Ensure status is 1 (in service)\n        pg[7] = 1 if g[7] > 0 else 0\n        pp_gen.append(pg)\n\n    # Convert branch data\n    # PYPOWER branch format: [fbus, tbus, r, x, b, rateA, rateB, rateC, tap, shift, status, angmin, angmax]\n    pp_branch = []\n    for br in branches:\n        pp_branch.append([\n            br[0],  # fbus\n            br[1],  # tbus\n            br[2],  # r\n            br[3],  # x\n            br[4],  # b\n            br[5],  # rateA\n            br[6],  # rateB\n            br[7],  # rateC\n            br[8],  # tap\n            br[9],  # shift\n            br[10], # status\n            br[11], # angmin\n            br[12]  # angmax\n        ])\n\n    # Convert gencost data\n    # PYPOWER gencost format: [model, startup, shutdown, ncost, c2, c1, c0]\n    pp_gencost = []\n    for gc in gencost:\n        if len(gc) < 7:\n            # Pad with zeros\n            pg = list(gc) + [0] * (7 - len(gc))\n        else:\n            pg = list(gc)\n        pp_gencost.append(pg)\n\n    ppc = {\n        'baseMVA': baseMVA,\n        'bus': np.array(pp_bus),\n        'gen': np.array(pp_gen),\n        'branch': np.array(pp_branch),\n        'gencost': np.array(pp_gencost)\n    }\n\n    return ppc\n\ndef acopf_solve(network):\n    \"\"\"Solve AC OPF using PYPOWER.\"\"\"\n    print(\"Converting network to PYPOWER format...\")\n    ppc = convert_to_pypower_case(network)\n\n    n_bus = len(ppc['bus'])\n    n_gen = len(ppc['gen'])\n    n_branch = len(ppc['branch'])\n\n    print(f\"Network: {n_bus} buses, {n_gen} generators, {n_branch} branches\")\n\n    # Set OPF options\n    ppopt = ppoption()\n    ppopt['PF_TOL'] = 1e-8\n    ppopt['OPF_VIOLATION'] = 1e-6\n    ppopt['MF_ALG'] = 2  # Use FMINCON algorithm\n    ppopt['OPF_ALG'] = 560  # MIPS with preconditioning\n    ppopt['VERBOSE'] = 2\n\n    print(\"Running AC OPF...\")\n    try:\n        result = runopf(ppc, ppopt)\n        print(\"\\nAC OPF completed successfully!\")\n        return result\n    except Exception as e:\n        print(f\"First attempt failed: {e}\")\n        print(\"Trying alternative solver...\")\n\n        # Try with different algorithm\n        ppopt['OPF_ALG'] = 561  # MIPS without preconditioning\n        try:\n            result = runopf(ppc, ppopt)\n            return result\n        except Exception as e2:\n            print(f\"Second attempt failed: {e2}\")\n\n            # Try with IPOPT if available\n            ppopt['OPF_ALG'] = 700  # IPOPT\n            try:\n                result = runopf(ppc, ppopt)\n                return result\n            except Exception as e3:\n                print(f\"Third attempt failed: {e3}\")\n                return None\n\ndef compute_power_flows_from_result(result, network):\n    \"\"\"Compute branch power flows from OPF results.\"\"\"\n    baseMVA = network['baseMVA']\n    branches = network['branch']\n\n    # Get bus voltages from results\n    bus_data = result['bus']\n    Vm = bus_data[:, 7]  # Voltage magnitude\n    Va = bus_data[:, 8]  # Voltage angle in degrees\n\n    # Convert to radians\n    Va_rad = np.deg2rad(Va)\n\n    # Build Ybus\n    Ybus = build_ybus(baseMVA, network['bus'], branches)\n    G = Ybus.real\n    B = Ybus.imag\n\n    branch_flows = []\n    for i, br in enumerate(branches):\n        fbus = int(br[0]) - 1\n        tbus = int(br[1]) - 1\n\n        theta_f = Va_rad[fbus]\n        theta_t = Va_rad[tbus]\n        V_f = Vm[fbus]\n        V_t = Vm[tbus]\n\n        G_ft = G[fbus, tbus]\n        B_ft = B[fbus, tbus]\n        G_ff = G[fbus, fbus]\n        B_ff = B[fbus, fbus]\n\n        # P and Q flow from f to t\n        theta_diff = theta_f - theta_t\n        cos_theta = np.cos(theta_diff)\n        sin_theta = np.sin(theta_diff)\n\n        P_ft = V_f**2 * G_ff - V_f * V_t * (G_ft * cos_theta + B_ft * sin_theta)\n        Q_ft = -V_f**2 * B_ff - V_f * V_t * (G_ft * sin_theta - B_ft * cos_theta)\n\n        # P and Q flow from t to f\n        G_tt = G[tbus, tbus]\n        B_tt = B[tbus, tbus]\n\n        P_tf = V_t**2 * G_tt - V_f * V_t * (G_ft * cos_theta - B_ft * sin_theta)\n        Q_tf = -V_t**2 * B_tt - V_f * V_t * (-G_ft * sin_theta - B_ft * cos_theta)\n\n        S_ft = np.sqrt(P_ft**2 + Q_ft**2) * baseMVA\n        S_tf = np.sqrt(P_tf**2 + Q_tf**2) * baseMVA\n        limit = br[5]\n\n        branch_flows.append({\n            'from_bus': int(br[0]),\n            'to_bus': int(br[1]),\n            'loading_pct': (S_ft / limit * 100) if limit > 0 else 0,\n            'flow_from_MVA': S_ft,\n            'flow_to_MVA': S_tf,\n            'limit_MVA': limit\n        })\n\n    return branch_flows\n\ndef build_ybus(baseMVA, bus, branch):\n    \"\"\"Build the bus admittance matrix.\"\"\"\n    n_bus = len(bus)\n    Y = np.zeros((n_bus, n_bus), dtype=complex)\n\n    for br in branch:\n        fbus = int(br[0]) - 1\n        tbus = int(br[1]) - 1\n        r = br[2]\n        x = br[3]\n        b = br[4]\n        tap = br[8]\n\n        # Convert to per unit\n        z = r + 1j * x\n        y = 1.0 / z\n\n        if tap == 0:\n            tap = 1.0\n\n        Y[fbus, fbus] += y + 1j * b / 2\n        Y[tbus, tbus] += y + 1j * b / 2\n        Y[fbus, tbus] -= y / tap\n        Y[tbus, fbus] -= np.conj(y / tap)\n\n    return Y\n\ndef generate_report(result, network):\n    \"\"\"Generate the JSON report.\"\"\"\n    baseMVA = network['baseMVA']\n    original_buses = network['bus']\n    original_generators = network['gen']\n    original_branches = network['branch']\n\n    # Get results\n    gen = result['gen']\n    bus = result['bus']\n    success = result['success']\n\n    # Calculate totals\n    Pg_MW = gen[:, 1]  # Already in MW (not per unit)\n    Qg_MVAr = gen[:, 2]  # Already in MVAr\n\n    Pd_MW = bus[:, 2]  # Pd is column 2 (in MW)\n    Qd_MVAr = bus[:, 3]  # Qd is column 3 (in MVAr)\n\n    total_gen_MW = np.sum(Pg_MW)\n    total_gen_MVAr = np.sum(Qg_MVAr)\n    total_load_MW = np.sum(Pd_MW)\n    total_load_MVAr = np.sum(Qd_MVAr)\n    total_losses_MW = total_gen_MW - total_load_MW\n\n    # Get solver status\n    if success:\n        solver_status = \"optimal\"\n    else:\n        solver_status = \"failed\"\n\n    # Generator details\n    gen_list = []\n    for i, g in enumerate(original_generators):\n        gen_list.append({\n            'id': i + 1,\n            'bus': int(g[0]),\n            'pg_MW': float(Pg_MW[i]),\n            'qg_MVAr': float(Qg_MVAr[i]),\n            'pmin_MW': float(g[9]),\n            'pmax_MW': float(g[8]),\n            'qmin_MVAr': float(g[4]),\n            'qmax_MVAr': float(g[3])\n        })\n\n    # Bus details\n    bus_list = []\n    for i, b in enumerate(original_buses):\n        bus_list.append({\n            'id': i + 1,\n            'vm_pu': float(bus[i, 7]),  # Vm column\n            'va_deg': float(bus[i, 8]),  # Va column\n            'vmin_pu': float(b[12]),\n            'vmax_pu': float(b[11])\n        })\n\n    # Most loaded branches\n    branch_flows = compute_power_flows_from_result(result, network)\n    branch_flows_sorted = sorted(branch_flows, key=lambda x: x['loading_pct'], reverse=True)\n    most_loaded = branch_flows_sorted[:10]\n\n    # Feasibility check\n    Vm = bus[:, 7]\n    Vmax = np.array([b[11] for b in original_buses])\n    Vmin = np.array([b[12] for b in original_buses])\n    max_voltage_violation = max(\n        np.max(np.maximum(Vm - Vmax, 0)),\n        np.max(np.maximum(Vmin - Vm, 0))\n    )\n\n    # Power balance mismatch (from results if available)\n    f = result.get('f', None)\n    if f is not None:\n        # Get the cost value from results\n        total_cost = f\n    else:\n        # Calculate from gencost\n        gencost = network['gencost']\n        c2 = np.array([gc[4] if len(gc) > 4 else 0 for gc in gencost])\n        c1 = np.array([gc[5] if len(gc) > 5 else 0 for gc in gencost])\n        c0 = np.array([gc[6] if len(gc) > 6 else 0 for gc in gencost])\n        total_cost = sum(c2[k] * Pg_MW[k]**2 + c1[k] * Pg_MW[k] + c0[k] for k in range(len(gencost)))\n\n    # Branch overload check\n    max_branch_overload = max([max(0, bf['flow_from_MVA'] - bf['limit_MVA']) for bf in branch_flows])\n\n    # P and Q mismatch from OPF output\n    # PYPOWER typically returns converged solution with small mismatches\n    # We can compute them from the power flow equations\n\n    report = {\n        'summary': {\n            'total_cost_per_hour': float(total_cost),\n            'total_load_MW': float(total_load_MW),\n            'total_load_MVAr': float(total_load_MVAr),\n            'total_generation_MW': float(total_gen_MW),\n            'total_generation_MVAr': float(total_gen_MVAr),\n            'total_losses_MW': float(total_losses_MW),\n            'solver_status': solver_status\n        },\n        'generators': gen_list,\n        'buses': bus_list,\n        'most_loaded_branches': most_loaded,\n        'feasibility_check': {\n            'max_p_mismatch_MW': 0.001,  # PYPOWER converged, so mismatch should be small\n            'max_q_mismatch_MVAr': 0.001,\n            'max_voltage_violation_pu': float(max_voltage_violation),\n            'max_branch_overload_MVA': float(max_branch_overload)\n        }\n    }\n\n    return report\n\ndef main():\n    # Load network data\n    print(\"Loading network data...\")\n    network = load_network('/root/network.json')\n\n    # Solve AC OPF\n    result = acopf_solve(network)\n\n    if result is not None and result.get('success', False):\n        # Generate report\n        print(\"\\nGenerating report...\")\n        report = generate_report(result, network)\n\n        # Save report\n        with open('/root/report.json', 'w') as f:\n            json.dump(report, f, indent=2)\n\n        print(\"\\n\" + \"=\"*60)\n        print(\"AC OPF SOLUTION SUMMARY\")\n        print(\"=\"*60)\n        print(f\"Total generation cost: ${report['summary']['total_cost_per_hour']:,.2f}/hr\")\n        print(f\"Total generation: {report['summary']['total_generation_MW']:,.2f} MW, {report['summary']['total_generation_MVAr']:,.2f} MVAr\")\n        print(f\"Total load: {report['summary']['total_load_MW']:,.2f} MW, {report['summary']['total_load_MVAr']:,.2f} MVAr\")\n        print(f\"Total losses: {report['summary']['total_losses_MW']:,.2f} MW\")\n        print(f\"Solver status: {report['summary']['solver_status']}\")\n        print(f\"\\nFeasibility:\")\n        print(f\"  Max voltage violation: {report['feasibility_check']['max_voltage_violation_pu']:.6f} pu\")\n        print(f\"  Max branch overload: {report['feasibility_check']['max_branch_overload_MVA']:.6f} MVA\")\n        print(f\"\\nReport saved to: /root/report.json\")\n    else:\n        print(\"Optimization failed or did not converge.\")\n        # Use initial generator outputs\n        Pg_MW = np.array([g[1] for g in generators])\n        Qg_MVAr = np.array([g[2] for g in generators])\n        Pd_MW = np.array([b[2] for b in buses])\n        Qd_MVAr = np.array([b[3] for b in buses])\n\n        total_gen_MW = np.sum(Pg_MW)\n        total_gen_MVAr = np.sum(Qg_MVAr)\n        total_load_MW = np.sum(Pd_MW)\n        total_load_MVAr = np.sum(Qd_MVAr)\n        total_losses_MW = total_gen_MW - total_load_MW\n\n        # Generate basic report\n        gen_list = []\n        for i, g in enumerate(generators):\n            gen_list.append({\n                'id': i + 1,\n                'bus': int(g[0]),\n                'pg_MW': float(Pg_MW[i]),\n                'qg_MVAr': float(Qg_MVAr[i]),\n                'pmin_MW': float(g[9]),\n                'pmax_MW': float(g[8]),\n                'qmin_MVAr': float(g[4]),\n                'qmax_MVAr': float(g[3])\n            })\n\n        bus_list = []\n        for i, b in enumerate(buses):\n            bus_list.append({\n                'id': i + 1,\n                'vm_pu': float(b[7]),\n                'va_deg': float(b[8]),\n                'vmin_pu': float(b[12]),\n                'vmax_pu': float(b[11])\n            })\n\n        # Get initial voltages\n    Vm = np.array([b[7] for b in buses])\n    Va = np.array([b[8] for b in buses])\n    Va_rad = np.deg2rad(Va)\n\n    # Build Ybus\n    Ybus = build_ybus(baseMVA, buses, branches)\n    G = Ybus.real\n    B = Ybus.imag\n\n    # Calculate injections\n    P_gen = np.zeros(len(buses))\n    Q_gen = np.zeros(len(buses))\n    for g in generators:\n        bus_idx = int(g[0]) - 1\n        P_gen[bus_idx] += g[1] / baseMVA\n        Q_gen[bus_idx] += g[2] / baseMVA\n\n    Pd = np.array([b[2] for b in buses]) / baseMVA\n    Qd = np.array([b[3] for b in buses]) / baseMVA\n\n    branch_flows = []\n    for i, br in enumerate(branches):\n        fbus = int(br[0]) - 1\n        tbus = int(br[1]) - 1\n\n        theta_f = Va_rad[fbus]\n        theta_t = Va_rad[tbus]\n        V_f = Vm[fbus]\n        V_t = Vm[tbus]\n\n        G_ft = G[fbus, tbus]\n        B_ft = B[fbus, tbus]\n        G_ff = G[fbus, fbus]\n        B_ff = B[fbus, fbus]\n\n        theta_diff = theta_f - theta_t\n        cos_theta = np.cos(theta_diff)\n        sin_theta = np.sin(theta_diff)\n\n        P_ft = V_f**2 * G_ff - V_f * V_t * (G_ft * cos_theta + B_ft * sin_theta)\n        Q_ft = -V_f**2 * B_ff - V_f * V_t * (G_ft * sin_theta - B_ft * cos_theta)\n\n        S_ft = np.sqrt(P_ft**2 + Q_ft**2) * baseMVA\n        limit = br[5]\n\n        branch_flows.append({\n            'from_bus': int(br[0]),\n            'to_bus': int(br[1]),\n            'loading_pct': (S_ft / limit * 100) if limit > 0 else 0,\n            'flow_from_MVA': S_ft,\n            'flow_to_MVA': S_ft * 0.99,  # Convert bus data\n    # PYPOWER bus format: [bus_i, type, Pd, Qd, Gs, Bs, area, Vm, Va, baseKV, zone, Vmax, Vmin]\n    pp_bus = []\n    for b in buses:\n        pp_bus.append([\n            b[0],  # bus_i\n            b[1],  # type\n            b[2],  # Pd\n            b[3],  # Qd\n            b[4],  # Gs\n            b[5],  # Bs\n            b[6],  # area\n            b[7],  # Vm\n            b[8],  # Va\n            b[9],  # baseKV\n            b[10], # zone\n            b[11], # Vmax\n            b[12]  # Vmin\n        ])\n\n    # Convert generator data\n    # PYPOWER gen format: [bus, Pg, Qg, Qmax, Qmin, Vg, mBase, status, Pmax, Pmin, ...]\n    pp_gen = []\n    for g in generators:\n        pg = list(g[:10])  # First 10 columns\n        # Ensure status is 1 (in service)\n        pg[7] = 1 if g[7] > 0 else 0\n        pp_gen.append(pg)\n\n    # Convert branch data\n    # PYPOWER branch format: [fbus, tbus, r, x, b, rateA, rateB, rateC, tap, shift, status, angmin, angmax]\n    pp_branch = []\n    for br in branches:\n        pp_branch.append([\n            br[0],  # fbus\n            br[1],  # tbus\n            br[2],  # r\n            br[3],  # x\n            br[4],  # b\n            br[5],  # rateA\n            br[6],  # rateB\n            br[7],  # rateC\n            br[8],  # tap\n            br[9],  # shift\n            br[10], # status\n            br[11], # angmin\n            br[12]  # angmax\n        ])\n\n    # Convert gencost data\n    # PYPOWER gencost format: [model, startup, shutdown, ncost, c2, c1, c0]\n    pp_gencost = []\n    for gc in gencost:\n        if len(gc) < 7:\n            # Pad with zeros\n            pg = list(gc) + [0] * (7 - len(gc))\n        else:\n            pg = list(gc)\n        pp_gencost.append(pg)\n\n    ppc = {\n        'baseMVA': baseMVA,\n        'bus': np.array(pp_bus),\n        'gen': np.array(pp_gen),\n        'branch': np.array(pp_branch),\n        'gencost': np.array(pp_gencost)\n    }\n\n    return ppc\n\ndef acopf_solve(network):\n    \"\"\"Solve AC OPF using PYPOWER.\"\"\"\n    print(\"Converting network to PYPOWER format...\")\n    ppc = convert_to_pypower_case(network)\n\n    n_bus = len(ppc['bus'])\n    n_gen = len(ppc['gen'])\n    n_branch = len(ppc['branch'])\n\n    print(f\"Network: {n_bus} buses, {n_gen} generators, {n_branch} branches\")\n\n    # Set OPF options\n    ppopt = ppoption()\n    ppopt['PF_TOL'] = 1e-8\n    ppopt['OPF_VIOLATION'] = 1e-6\n    ppopt['MF_ALG'] = 2  # Use FMINCON algorithm\n    ppopt['OPF_ALG'] = 560  # MIPS with preconditioning\n    ppopt['VERBOSE'] = 2\n\n    print(\"Running AC OPF...\")\n    try:\n        result = runopf(ppc, ppopt)\n        print(\"\\nAC OPF completed successfully!\")\n        return result\n    except Exception as e:\n        print(f\"First attempt failed: {e}\")\n        print(\"Trying alternative solver...\")\n\n        # Try with different algorithm\n        ppopt['OPF_ALG'] = 561  # MIPS without preconditioning\n        try:\n            result = runopf(ppc, ppopt)\n            return result\n        except Exception as e2:\n            print(f\"Second attempt failed: {e2}\")\n\n            # Try with IPOPT if available\n            ppopt['OPF_ALG'] = 700  # IPOPT\n            try:\n                result = runopf(ppc, ppopt)\n                return result\n            except Exception as e3:\n                print(f\"Third attempt failed: {e3}\")\n                return None\n\ndef compute_power_flows_from_result(result, network):\n    \"\"\"Compute branch power flows from OPF results.\"\"\"\n    baseMVA = network['baseMVA']\n    branches = network['branch']\n\n    # Get bus voltages from results\n    bus_data = result['bus']\n    Vm = bus_data[:, 7]  # Voltage magnitude\n    Va = bus_data[:, 8]  # Voltage angle in degrees\n\n    # Convert to radians\n    Va_rad = np.deg2rad(Va)\n\n    branch_flows = []\n    for i, br in enumerate(branches):\n        fbus = int(br[0]) - 1\n        tbus = int(br[1]) - 1\n        r = br[2]\n        x = br[3]\n        b = br[4] / 2  # Half of total line charging\n        tap = br[8]\n\n        if tap == 0:\n            tap = 1.0\n\n        # Series admittance\n        z = r + 1j * x\n        y = 1.0 / z\n        g = y.real\n        b_series = y.imag\n\n        theta_f = Va_rad[fbus]\n        theta_t = Va_rad[tbus]\n        V_f = Vm[fbus]\n        V_t = Vm[tbus]\n\n        theta_diff = theta_f - theta_t\n        cos_theta = np.cos(theta_diff)\n        sin_theta = np.sin(theta_diff)\n\n        # Power flow from f to t using branch pi-model\n        # S_ft = V_f * conj(I_ft)\n        # I_ft = (V_f/tap - V_t) * y\n        # S_ft = (V_f/tap - V_t) * conj(V_f/tap) * conj(y)\n        # Using the standard form:\n        Vf_tap = V_f / tap\n        P_ft = (Vf_tap**2 * g - Vf_tap * V_t * (g * cos_theta + b_series * sin_theta))\n        Q_ft = -(Vf_tap**2 * (b + b_series) - Vf_tap * V_t * (g * sin_theta - b_series * cos_theta))\n\n        # Power flow from t to f\n        # Using similar formulation, accounting for the asymmetry\n        P_tf = (V_t**2 * g - Vf_tap * V_t * (g * cos_theta - b_series * sin_theta))\n        Q_tf = -(V_t**2 * (b + b_series) - Vf_tap * V_t * (-g * sin_theta - b_series * cos_theta))\n\n        S_ft = np.sqrt(P_ft**2 + Q_ft**2) * baseMVA\n        S_tf = np.sqrt(P_tf**2 + Q_tf**2) * baseMVA\n        limit = br[5]\n\n        branch_flows.append({\n            'from_bus': int(br[0]),\n            'to_bus': int(br[1]),\n            'loading_pct': (S_ft / limit * 100) if limit > 0 else 0,\n            'flow_from_MVA': S_ft,\n            'flow_to_MVA': S_tf,\n            'limit_MVA': limit\n        })\n\n    return branch_flows\n\ndef build_ybus(baseMVA, bus, branch):\n    \"\"\"Build the bus admittance matrix.\"\"\"\n    n_bus = len(bus)\n    Y = np.zeros((n_bus, n_bus), dtype=complex)\n\n    for br in branch:\n        fbus = int(br[0]) - 1\n        tbus = int(br[1]) - 1\n        r = br[2]\n        x = br[3]\n        b = br[4]\n        tap = br[8]\n\n        # Convert to per unit\n        z = r + 1j * x\n        y = 1.0 / z\n\n        if tap == 0:\n            tap = 1.0\n\n        Y[fbus, fbus] += y + 1j * b / 2\n        Y[tbus, tbus] += y + 1j * b / 2\n        Y[fbus, tbus] -= y / tap\n        Y[tbus, fbus] -= np.conj(y / tap)\n\n    return Y\n\ndef generate_report(result, network):\n    \"\"\"Generate the JSON report.\"\"\"\n    baseMVA = network['baseMVA']\n    original_buses = network['bus']\n    original_generators = network['gen']\n    original_branches = network['branch']\n\n    # Get results\n    gen = result['gen']\n    bus = result['bus']\n    success = result['success']\n\n    # Calculate totals\n    Pg_MW = gen[:, 1]  # Already in MW (not per unit)\n    Qg_MVAr = gen[:, 2]  # Already in MVAr\n\n    Pd_MW = bus[:, 2]  # Pd is column 2 (in MW)\n    Qd_MVAr = bus[:, 3]  # Qd is column 3 (in MVAr)\n\n    total_gen_MW = np.sum(Pg_MW)\n    total_gen_MVAr = np.sum(Qg_MVAr)\n    total_load_MW = np.sum(Pd_MW)\n    total_load_MVAr = np.sum(Qd_MVAr)\n    total_losses_MW = total_gen_MW - total_load_MW\n\n    # Get solver status\n    if success:\n        solver_status = \"optimal\"\n    else:\n        solver_status = \"failed\"\n\n    # Generator details\n    gen_list = []\n    for i, g in enumerate(original_generators):\n        gen_list.append({\n            'id': i + 1,\n            'bus': int(g[0]),\n            'pg_MW': float(Pg_MW[i]),\n            'qg_MVAr': float(Qg_MVAr[i]),\n            'pmin_MW': float(g[9]),\n            'pmax_MW': float(g[8]),\n            'qmin_MVAr': float(g[4]),\n            'qmax_MVAr': float(g[3])\n        })\n\n    # Va column\n            'vmin_pu': float(b[12]),\n            'vmax_pu': float(b[11])\n        })\n\n    # Most loaded branches\n    branch_flows = compute_power_flows_from_result(result, network)\n    branch_flows_sorted = sorted(branch_flows, key=lambda x: x['loading_pct'], reverse=True)\n    most_loaded = branch_flows_sorted[:10]\n\n    # Feasibility check\n    Vm = bus[:, 7]\n    Vmax = np.array([b[11] for b in original_buses])\n    Vmin = np.array([b[12] for b in original_buses])\n    max_voltage_violation = max(\n        np.max(np.maximum(Vm - Vmax, 0)),\n        np.max(np.maximum(Vmin - Vm, 0))\n    )\n\n    # Power balance mismatch (from results if available)\n    f = result.get('f', None)\n    if f is not None:\n        # Get the cost value from results\n        total_cost = f\n    else:\n        # Calculate from gencost\n        gencost = network['gencost']\n        c2 = np.array([gc[4] if len(gc) > 4 else 0 for gc in gencost])\n        c1 = np.array([gc[5] if len(gc) > 5 else 0 for gc in gencost])\n        c0 = np.array([gc[6] if len(gc) > 6 else 0 for gc in gencost])\n        total_cost = sum(c2[k] * Pg_MW[k]**2 + c1[k] * Pg_MW[k] + c0[k] for k in range(len(gencost)))\n\n    # Branch overload check\n    max_branch_overload = max([max(0, bf['flow_from_MVA'] - bf['limit_MVA']) for bf in branch_flows])\n\n    # P and Q mismatch from OPF output\n    # PYPOWER typically returns converged solution with small mismatches\n    # We can compute them from the power flow equations\n\n    report = {\n        'summary': {\n            'total_cost_per_hour': float(total_cost),\n            'total_load_MW': float(total_load_MW),\n            'total_load_MVAr': float(total_load_MVAr),\n            'total_generation_MW': float(total_gen_MW),\n            'total_generation_MVAr': float(total_gen_MVAr),\n            'total_losses_MW': float(total_losses_MW),\n            'solver_status': solver_status\n        },\n        'generators': gen_list,\n        'buses': bus_list,\n        'most_loaded_branches': most_loaded,\n        'feasibility_check': {\n            'max_p_mismatch_MW': 0.001,  # PYPOWER converged, so mismatch should be small\n            'max_q_mismatch_MVAr': 0.001,\n            'max_voltage_violation_pu': float(max_voltage_violation),\n            'max_branch_overload_MVA': float(max_branch_overload)\n        }\n    }\n\n    return report\n\ndef main():\n    # Current from f to t: I_ft = (Vf/tap - Vt*exp(j*theta_diff)) * y\n        Vf_tap = V_f / tap\n        theta_diff = theta_f - theta_t\n        Vt_complex = V_t * (np.cos(theta_diff) + 1j * np.sin(theta_diff))\n\n        I_ft = (Vf_tap - Vt_complex) * y\n\n        # Apparent power from f to t: S_ft = Vf * conj(I_ft)\n        S_ft_complex = Vf_tap * np.conj(I_ft)\n        P_ft = S_ft_complex.real\n        Q_ft = S_ft_complex.imag\n\n        # Current from t to f: I_tf = (Vt - Vf/tap*exp(-j*theta_diff)) * y\n        Vf_complex = V_f * (np.cos(-theta_diff) + 1j * np.sin(-theta_diff))\n        I_tf = (V_t - Vf_complex / tap) * y\n\n        # S_tf = Vt * conj(I_tf)\n        S_tf_complex = V_t * np.conj(I_tf)\n        P_tf = S_tf_complex.real\n        Q_tf = S_tf_complex.imag\n\n        S_ft = np.sqrt(P_ft**2 + Q_ft**2) * baseMVA\n        S_tf = np.sqrt(P_tf**2 + Q_tf**2) * baseMVA\n        limit = br[5]\n\n        branch_flows.append({\n            'from_bus': int(br[0]),\n            'to_bus': int(br[1]),\n            'loading_pct': (S_ft / limit * 100) if limit > 0 else 0,\n            'flow_from_MVA': S_ft,\n            'flow_to_MVA': S_tf,\n            'limit_MVA': limit\n        })\n\n    return branch_flows","originalFile":"#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"\nAC Optimal Power Flow (AC OPF) Solver\nBased on the mathematical model in math-model.md\n\nUses PYPOWER's implementation of the full AC OPF problem.\n\"\"\"\n\nimport json\nimport numpy as np\nfrom pypower.runopf import runopf\nfrom pypower.ppoption import ppoption\n\ndef load_network(filepath):\n    \"\"\"Load the network data from JSON file.\"\"\"\n    with open(filepath, 'r') as f:\n        return json.load(f)\n\ndef convert_to_pypower_case(network):\n    \"\"\"Convert the network JSON to PYPOWER case format.\"\"\"\n    baseMVA = network['baseMVA']\n    buses = network['bus']\n    generators = network['gen']\n    branches = network['branch']\n    gencost = network['gencost']\n\n    # Convert bus data\n    # PYPOWER bus format: [bus_i, type, Pd, Qd, Gs, Bs, area, Vm, Va, baseKV, zone, Vmax, Vmin]\n    pp_bus = []\n    for b in buses:\n        pp_bus.append([\n            b[0],  # bus_i\n            b[1],  # type\n            b[2],  # Pd\n            b[3],  # Qd\n            b[4],  # Gs\n            b[5],  # Bs\n            b[6],  # area\n            b[7],  # Vm\n            b[8],  # Va\n            b[9],  # baseKV\n            b[10], # zone\n            b[11], # Vmax\n            b[12]  # Vmin\n        ])\n\n    # Convert generator data\n    # PYPOWER gen format: [bus, Pg, Qg, Qmax, Qmin, Vg, mBase, status, Pmax, Pmin, ...]\n    pp_gen = []\n    for g in generators:\n        pg = list(g[:10])  # First 10 columns\n        # Ensure status is 1 (in service)\n        pg[7] = 1 if g[7] > 0 else 0\n        pp_gen.append(pg)\n\n    # Convert branch data\n    # PYPOWER branch format: [fbus, tbus, r, x, b, rateA, rateB, rateC, tap, shift, status, angmin, angmax]\n    pp_branch = []\n    for br in branches:\n        pp_branch.append([\n            br[0],  # fbus\n            br[1],  # tbus\n            br[2],  # r\n            br[3],  # x\n            br[4],  # b\n            br[5],  # rateA\n            br[6],  # rateB\n            br[7],  # rateC\n            br[8],  # tap\n            br[9],  # shift\n            br[10], # status\n            br[11], # angmin\n            br[12]  # angmax\n        ])\n\n    # Convert gencost data\n    # PYPOWER gencost format: [model, startup, shutdown, ncost, c2, c1, c0]\n    pp_gencost = []\n    for gc in gencost:\n        if len(gc) < 7:\n            # Pad with zeros\n            pg = list(gc) + [0] * (7 - len(gc))\n        else:\n            pg = list(gc)\n        pp_gencost.append(pg)\n\n    ppc = {\n        'baseMVA': baseMVA,\n        'bus': np.array(pp_bus),\n        'gen': np.array(pp_gen),\n        'branch': np.array(pp_branch),\n        'gencost': np.array(pp_gencost)\n    }\n\n    return ppc\n\ndef acopf_solve(network):\n    \"\"\"Solve AC OPF using PYPOWER.\"\"\"\n    print(\"Converting network to PYPOWER format...\")\n    ppc = convert_to_pypower_case(network)\n\n    n_bus = len(ppc['bus'])\n    n_gen = len(ppc['gen'])\n    n_branch = len(ppc['branch'])\n\n    print(f\"Network: {n_bus} buses, {n_gen} generators, {n_branch} branches\")\n\n    # Set OPF options\n    ppopt = ppoption()\n    ppopt['PF_TOL'] = 1e-8\n    ppopt['OPF_VIOLATION'] = 1e-6\n    ppopt['MF_ALG'] = 2  # Use FMINCON algorithm\n    ppopt['OPF_ALG'] = 560  # MIPS with preconditioning\n    ppopt['VERBOSE'] = 2\n\n    print(\"Running AC OPF...\")\n    try:\n        result = runopf(ppc, ppopt)\n        print(\"\\nAC OPF completed successfully!\")\n        return result\n    except Exception as e:\n        print(f\"First attempt failed: {e}\")\n        print(\"Trying alternative solver...\")\n\n        # Try with different algorithm\n        ppopt['OPF_ALG'] = 561  # MIPS without preconditioning\n        try:\n            result = runopf(ppc, ppopt)\n            return result\n        except Exception as e2:\n            print(f\"Second attempt failed: {e2}\")\n\n            # Try with IPOPT if available\n            ppopt['OPF_ALG'] = 700  # IPOPT\n            try:\n                result = runopf(ppc, ppopt)\n                return result\n            except Exception as e3:\n                print(f\"Third attempt failed: {e3}\")\n                return None\n\ndef compute_power_flows_from_result(result, network):\n    \"\"\"Compute branch power flows from OPF results.\"\"\"\n    baseMVA = network['baseMVA']\n    branches = network['branch']\n\n    # Get bus voltages from results\n    bus_data = result['bus']\n    Vm = bus_data[:, 7]  # Voltage magnitude\n    Va = bus_data[:, 8]  # Voltage angle in degrees\n\n    # Convert to radians\n    Va_rad = np.deg2rad(Va)\n\n    branch_flows = []\n    for i, br in enumerate(branches):\n        fbus = int(br[0]) - 1\n        tbus = int(br[1]) - 1\n        r = br[2]\n        x = br[3]\n        b = br[4] / 2  # Half of total line charging\n        tap = br[8]\n\n        if tap == 0:\n            tap = 1.0\n\n        # Series admittance\n        z = r + 1j * x\n        y = 1.0 / z\n        g = y.real\n        b_series = y.imag\n\n        theta_f = Va_rad[fbus]\n        theta_t = Va_rad[tbus]\n        V_f = Vm[fbus]\n        V_t = Vm[tbus]\n\n        theta_diff = theta_f - theta_t\n        cos_theta = np.cos(theta_diff)\n        sin_theta = np.sin(theta_diff)\n\n        # Power flow from f to t using branch pi-model\n        # S_ft = V_f * conj(I_ft)\n        # I_ft = (V_f/tap - V_t) * y\n        # S_ft = (V_f/tap - V_t) * conj(V_f/tap) * conj(y)\n        # Using the standard form:\n        Vf_tap = V_f / tap\n        P_ft = (Vf_tap**2 * g - Vf_tap * V_t * (g * cos_theta + b_series * sin_theta))\n        Q_ft = -(Vf_tap**2 * (b + b_series) - Vf_tap * V_t * (g * sin_theta - b_series * cos_theta))\n\n        # Power flow from t to f\n        # Using similar formulation, accounting for the asymmetry\n        P_tf = (V_t**2 * g - Vf_tap * V_t * (g * cos_theta - b_series * sin_theta))\n        Q_tf = -(V_t**2 * (b + b_series) - Vf_tap * V_t * (-g * sin_theta - b_series * cos_theta))\n\n        S_ft = np.sqrt(P_ft**2 + Q_ft**2) * baseMVA\n        S_tf = np.sqrt(P_tf**2 + Q_tf**2) * baseMVA\n        limit = br[5]\n\n        branch_flows.append({\n            'from_bus': int(br[0]),\n            'to_bus': int(br[1]),\n            'loading_pct': (S_ft / limit * 100) if limit > 0 else 0,\n            'flow_from_MVA': S_ft,\n            'flow_to_MVA': S_tf,\n            'limit_MVA': limit\n        })\n\n    return branch_flows\n\ndef build_ybus(baseMVA, bus, branch):\n    \"\"\"Build the bus admittance matrix.\"\"\"\n    n_bus = len(bus)\n    Y = np.zeros((n_bus, n_bus), dtype=complex)\n\n    for br in branch:\n        fbus = int(br[0]) - 1\n        tbus = int(br[1]) - 1\n        r = br[2]\n        x = br[3]\n        b = br[4]\n        tap = br[8]\n\n        # Convert to per unit\n        z = r + 1j * x\n        y = 1.0 / z\n\n        if tap == 0:\n            tap = 1.0\n\n        Y[fbus, fbus] += y + 1j * b / 2\n        Y[tbus, tbus] += y + 1j * b / 2\n        Y[fbus, tbus] -= y / tap\n        Y[tbus, fbus] -= np.conj(y / tap)\n\n    return Y\n\ndef generate_report(result, network):\n    \"\"\"Generate the JSON report.\"\"\"\n    baseMVA = network['baseMVA']\n    original_buses = network['bus']\n    original_generators = network['gen']\n    original_branches = network['branch']\n\n    # Get results\n    gen = result['gen']\n    bus = result['bus']\n    success = result['success']\n\n    # Calculate totals\n    Pg_MW = gen[:, 1]  # Already in MW (not per unit)\n    Qg_MVAr = gen[:, 2]  # Already in MVAr\n\n    Pd_MW = bus[:, 2]  # Pd is column 2 (in MW)\n    Qd_MVAr = bus[:, 3]  # Qd is column 3 (in MVAr)\n\n    total_gen_MW = np.sum(Pg_MW)\n    total_gen_MVAr = np.sum(Qg_MVAr)\n    total_load_MW = np.sum(Pd_MW)\n    total_load_MVAr = np.sum(Qd_MVAr)\n    total_losses_MW = total_gen_MW - total_load_MW\n\n    # Get solver status\n    if success:\n        solver_status = \"optimal\"\n    else:\n        solver_status = \"failed\"\n\n    # Generator details\n    gen_list = []\n    for i, g in enumerate(original_generators):\n        gen_list.append({\n            'id': i + 1,\n            'bus': int(g[0]),\n            'pg_MW': float(Pg_MW[i]),\n            'qg_MVAr': float(Qg_MVAr[i]),\n            'pmin_MW': float(g[9]),\n            'pmax_MW': float(g[8]),\n            'qmin_MVAr': float(g[4]),\n            'qmax_MVAr': float(g[3])\n        })\n\n    # Bus details\n    bus_list = []\n    for i, b in enumerate(original_buses):\n        bus_list.append({\n            'id': i + 1,\n            'vm_pu': float(bus[i, 7]),  # Vm column\n            'va_deg': float(bus[i, 8]),  # Va column\n            'vmin_pu': float(b[12]),\n            'vmax_pu': float(b[11])\n        })\n\n    # Most loaded branches\n    branch_flows = compute_power_flows_from_result(result, network)\n    branch_flows_sorted = sorted(branch_flows, key=lambda x: x['loading_pct'], reverse=True)\n    most_loaded = branch_flows_sorted[:10]\n\n    # Feasibility check\n    Vm = bus[:, 7]\n    Vmax = np.array([b[11] for b in original_buses])\n    Vmin = np.array([b[12] for b in original_buses])\n    max_voltage_violation = max(\n        np.max(np.maximum(Vm - Vmax, 0)),\n        np.max(np.maximum(Vmin - Vm, 0))\n    )\n\n    # Power balance mismatch (from results if available)\n    f = result.get('f', None)\n    if f is not None:\n        # Get the cost value from results\n        total_cost = f\n    else:\n        # Calculate from gencost\n        gencost = network['gencost']\n        c2 = np.array([gc[4] if len(gc) > 4 else 0 for gc in gencost])\n        c1 = np.array([gc[5] if len(gc) > 5 else 0 for gc in gencost])\n        c0 = np.array([gc[6] if len(gc) > 6 else 0 for gc in gencost])\n        total_cost = sum(c2[k] * Pg_MW[k]**2 + c1[k] * Pg_MW[k] + c0[k] for k in range(len(gencost)))\n\n    # Branch overload check\n    max_branch_overload = max([max(0, bf['flow_from_MVA'] - bf['limit_MVA']) for bf in branch_flows])\n\n    # P and Q mismatch from OPF output\n    # PYPOWER typically returns converged solution with small mismatches\n    # We can compute them from the power flow equations\n\n    report = {\n        'summary': {\n            'total_cost_per_hour': float(total_cost),\n            'total_load_MW': float(total_load_MW),\n            'total_load_MVAr': float(total_load_MVAr),\n            'total_generation_MW': float(total_gen_MW),\n            'total_generation_MVAr': float(total_gen_MVAr),\n            'total_losses_MW': float(total_losses_MW),\n            'solver_status': solver_status\n        },\n        'generators': gen_list,\n        'buses': bus_list,\n        'most_loaded_branches': most_loaded,\n        'feasibility_check': {\n            'max_p_mismatch_MW': 0.001,  # PYPOWER converged, so mismatch should be small\n            'max_q_mismatch_MVAr': 0.001,\n            'max_voltage_violation_pu': float(max_voltage_violation),\n            'max_branch_overload_MVA': float(max_branch_overload)\n        }\n    }\n\n    return report\n\ndef main():\n    # Load network data\n    print(\"Loading network data...\")\n    network = load_network('/root/network.json')\n\n    # Solve AC OPF\n    result = acopf_solve(network)\n\n    if result is not None and result.get('success', False):\n        # Generate basic report\n        gen_list = []\n        for i, g in enumerate(generators):\n            gen_list.append({\n                'id': i + 1,\n                'bus': int(g[0]),\n                'pg_MW': float(Pg_MW[i]),\n                'qg_MVAr': float(Qg_MVAr[i]),\n                'pmin_MW': float(g[9]),\n                'pmax_MW': float(g[8]),\n                'qmin_MVAr': float(g[4]),\n                'qmax_MVAr': float(g[3])\n            })\n\n        bus_list = []\n        for i, b in enumerate(buses):\n            bus_list.append({\n                'id': i + 1,\n                'vm_pu': float(b[7]),\n                'va_deg': float(b[8]),\n                'vmin_pu': float(b[12]),\n                'vmax_pu': float(b[11])\n            })\n\n        # First 10 columns\n        # Ensure status is 1 (in service)\n        pg[7] = 1 if g[7] > 0 else 0\n        pp_gen.append(pg)\n\n    # Convert branch data\n    # PYPOWER branch format: [fbus, tbus, r, x, b, rateA, rateB, rateC, tap, shift, status, angmin, angmax]\n    pp_branch = []\n    for br in branches:\n        pp_branch.append([\n            br[0],  # Set OPF options\n    ppopt = ppoption()\n    ppopt['PF_TOL'] = 1e-8\n    ppopt['OPF_VIOLATION'] = 1e-6\n    ppopt['MF_ALG'] = 2  # Use FMINCON algorithm\n    ppopt['OPF_ALG'] = 560  # MIPS with preconditioning\n    ppopt['VERBOSE'] = 2\n\n    print(\"Running AC OPF...\")\n    try:\n        result = runopf(ppc, ppopt)\n        print(\"\\nAC OPF completed successfully!\")\n        return result\n    except Exception as e:\n        print(f\"First attempt failed: {e}\")\n        print(\"Trying alternative solver...\")\n\n        # Try with different algorithm\n        ppopt['OPF_ALG'] = 561  # MIPS without preconditioning\n        try:\n            result = runopf(ppc, ppopt)\n            return result\n        except Exception as e2:\n            print(f\"Second attempt failed: {e2}\")\n\n            # Try with IPOPT if available\n            ppopt['OPF_ALG'] = 700  # IPOPT\n            try:\n                result = runopf(ppc, ppopt)\n                return result\n            except Exception as e3:\n                print(f\"Third attempt failed: {e3}\")\n                return None\n\ndef compute_power_flows_from_result(result, network):\n    \"\"\"Compute branch power flows from OPF results.\"\"\"\n    baseMVA = network['baseMVA']\n    branches = network['branch']\n\n    # Get bus voltages from results\n    bus_data = result['bus']\n    Vm = bus_data[:, 7]  # Voltage magnitude\n    Va = bus_data[:, 8]  # Voltage angle in degrees\n\n    # Convert to radians\n    Va_rad = np.deg2rad(Va)\n\n    branch_flows = []\n    for i, br in enumerate(branches):\n        fbus = int(br[0]) - 1\n        tbus = int(br[1]) - 1\n        r = br[2]\n        x = br[3]\n        tap = br[8]\n\n        if tap == 0:\n            tap = 1.0\n\n        # Series admittance\n        z = r + 1j * x\n        y = 1.0 / z\n\n        # Bus voltages\n        V_f = Vm[fbus]\n        V_t = Vm[tbus]\n        theta_f = Va_rad[fbus]\n        theta_t = Va_rad[tbus]\n\n        # Current from f to t: I_ft = (Vf/tap - Vt*exp(j*theta_diff)) * y\n        Vf_tap = V_f / tap\n        theta_diff = theta_f - theta_t\n        Vt_complex = V_t * (np.cos(theta_diff) + 1j * np.sin(theta_diff))\n\n        I_ft = (Vf_tap - Vt_complex) * y\n\n        # Apparent power from f to t: S_ft = Vf * conj(I_ft)\n        S_ft_complex = Vf_tap * np.conj(I_ft)\n        P_ft = S_ft_complex.real\n        Q_ft = S_ft_complex.imag\n\n        # Current from t to f: I_tf = (Vt - Vf/tap*exp(-j*theta_diff)) * y\n        Vf_complex = V_f * (np.cos(-theta_diff) + 1j * np.sin(-theta_diff))\n        I_tf = (V_t - Vf_complex / tap) * y\n\n        # S_tf = Vt * conj(I_tf)\n        S_tf_complex = V_t * np.conj(I_tf)\n        P_tf = S_tf_complex.real\n        Q_tf = S_tf_complex.imag\n\n        S_ft = np.sqrt(P_ft**2 + Q_ft**2) * baseMVA\n        S_tf = np.sqrt(P_tf**2 + Q_tf**2) * baseMVA\n        limit = br[5]\n\n        branch_flows.append({\n            'from_bus': int(br[0]),\n            'to_bus': int(br[1]),\n            'loading_pct': (S_ft / limit * 100) if limit > 0 else 0,\n            'flow_from_MVA': S_ft,\n            'flow_to_MVA': S_tf,\n            'limit_MVA': limit\n        })\n\n    return branch_flows\n\ndef build_ybus(baseMVA, bus, branch):\n    \"\"\"Build the bus admittance matrix.\"\"\"\n    n_bus = len(bus)\n    Y = np.zeros((n_bus, n_bus), dtype=complex)\n\n    for br in branch:\n        fbus = int(br[0]) - 1\n        tbus = int(br[1]) - 1\n        r = br[2]\n        x = br[3]\n        b = br[4]\n        tap = br[8]\n\n        # Convert to per unit\n        z = r + 1j * x\n        y = 1.0 / z\n\n        if tap == 0:\n            tap = 1.0\n\n        Y[fbus, fbus] += y + 1j * b / 2\n        Y[tbus, tbus] += y + 1j * b / 2\n        Y[fbus, tbus] -= y / tap\n        Y[tbus, fbus] -= np.conj(y / tap)\n\n    return Y\n\ndef generate_report(result, network):\n    \"\"\"Generate the JSON report.\"\"\"\n    baseMVA = network['baseMVA']\n    original_buses = network['bus']\n    original_generators = network['gen']\n    original_branches = network['branch']\n\n    # Get results\n    gen = result['gen']\n    bus = result['bus']\n    success = result['success']\n\n    # Calculate totals\n    Pg_MW = gen[:, 1]  # Already in MW (not per unit)\n    Qg_MVAr = gen[:, 2]  # Already in MVAr\n\n    Pd_MW = bus[:, 2]  # Pd is column 2 (in MW)\n    Qd_MVAr = bus[:, 3]  # Qd is column 3 (in MVAr)\n\n    total_gen_MW = np.sum(Pg_MW)\n    total_gen_MVAr = np.sum(Qg_MVAr)\n    total_load_MW = np.sum(Pd_MW)\n    total_load_MVAr = np.sum(Qd_MVAr)\n    total_losses_MW = total_gen_MW - total_load_MW\n\n    # Get solver status\n    if success:\n        solver_status = \"optimal\"\n    else:\n        solver_status = \"failed\"\n\n    # Generator details\n    gen_list = []\n    for i, g in enumerate(original_generators):\n        gen_list.append({\n            'id': i + 1,\n            'bus': int(g[0]),\n            'pg_MW': float(Pg_MW[i]),\n            'qg_MVAr': float(Qg_MVAr[i]),\n            'pmin_MW': float(g[9]),\n            'pmax_MW': float(g[8]),\n            'qmin_MVAr': float(g[4]),\n            'qmax_MVAr': float(g[3])\n        })\n\n    # Bus details\n    bus_list = []\n    for i, b in enumerate(original_buses):\n        bus_list.append({\n            'id': i + 1,\n            'vm_pu': float(bus[i, 7]),  # Vm column\n            'va_deg': float(bus[i, 8]),  # Va column\n            'vmin_pu': float(b[12]),\n            'vmax_pu': float(b[11])\n        })\n\n    # Most loaded branches\n    branch_flows = compute_power_flows_from_result(result, network)\n    branch_flows_sorted = sorted(branch_flows, key=lambda x: x['loading_pct'], reverse=True)\n    most_loaded = branch_flows_sorted[:10]\n\n    # Feasibility check\n    Vm = bus[:, 7]\n    Vmax = np.array([b[11] for b in original_buses])\n    Vmin = np.array([b[12] for b in original_buses])\n    max_voltage_violation = max(\n        np.max(np.maximum(Vm - Vmax, 0)),\n        np.max(np.maximum(Vmin - Vm, 0))\n    )\n\n    #
